# ZORO MEMORY BRAIN V3 — DEEP HISTORY READY

Version: 3.0
Owner: Muhammad Mubashar
Brand: Pasha Dev
Assistant: Zoro
Purpose: Deep personal-memory seed + history-ingestion-ready knowledge base

## IMPORTANT SOURCE BOUNDARY

This document combines the available V1/V2 Zoro memory material and expands it into a more structured temporal and behavioral memory layer.
A complete ChatGPT account export was not found in the accessible Library search during this build.
Therefore this document does NOT pretend to contain every conversation since 2022/2023.
When a real ChatGPT export is supplied, it should be ingested into this structure and historical memories should be linked to actual conversation timestamps and source messages.
Do not fabricate missing historical conversations.

## 00 — INGESTION CONTRACT

1. Parse exported conversations into conversation_id, message_id, timestamp, role, text, attachments, and title where available.
2. Normalize duplicate conversations without destroying original chronology.
3. Extract explicit user facts separately from assistant suggestions.
4. Extract preferences separately from goals.
5. Extract project decisions separately from implementation claims.
6. Preserve historical values with timestamps.
7. Mark contradictions instead of silently merging incompatible facts.
8. Prefer explicit corrections over older memories.
9. Never turn a model-generated assumption into a user fact.
10. Link every imported memory to its source conversation/message whenever the export supports it.
11. Generate summaries only after raw historical evidence has been retained.
12. Keep raw conversation archive separate from semantic memory.
13. Allow user-requested deletion/deactivation of memories.
14. Support re-indexing when new exports are imported.
15. Keep current repository state above stale project memories for implementation questions.

## 01 — TEMPORAL HISTORY INDEX

### 2022–2023
- The user wants this period represented when a real ChatGPT export is supplied; no complete export was available in the accessible file library during this build.

### 2026-01
- YouTube was discussed as having started in January 2026; subscriber counts mentioned at different times are historical snapshots.

### 2026-05
- AMD Developer Hackathon participation was discussed, including cloud-credit and prize-pool context; treat those details as historical.

### 2026-07
- RM MOBILE SHOP client work was discussed, including roughly 20 products and Hostinger deployment context.

### 2026-07
- A cinematic birthday website project was debugged around zoom/cropping, missing background images, black-screen behavior, Vercel/Nitro configuration, and localStorage issues.

### 2026-08
- The user continued premium website/template work, including birthday, anniversary, wedding, corporate, story, slowly, and celebration experiences.

### 2026-08
- The user moved toward a clean Zoro 2.0 rebuild after an older implementation became messy.

### 2026-08
- Zoro architecture was refined toward Tauri + React + Python Core, with native Windows behavior as a hard requirement.

### 2026-09
- A structured Zoro Memory Brain was created from currently retained context; this V3 expands that seed and prepares it for eventual true history ingestion.

## 99 — RELATIONSHIP GRAPH

- Muhammad Mubashar -> creator brand -> Pasha Dev.
- Pasha Dev -> social/content identity -> @pasha_dev_.
- Muhammad Mubashar -> preferred assistant -> Zoro.
- Zoro -> native Windows desktop assistant -> Tauri/React/Python Core direction.
- Zoro -> persistent memory -> SQLite.
- Zoro -> voice -> wake word, STT, TTS, VAD, barge-in.
- Zoro -> automation -> Windows/files/browser/terminal/IDE with permissions.
- User design preference -> premium/cinematic/immersive/high-end.
- User development preference -> real implementation + testing + verification.
- User communication preference -> Roman Urdu/Urdu/English mixed language.

## 100 — MEMORY RETRIEVAL POLICY

- For a coding task, retrieve technical profile + relevant project + current repo state + workflow preferences.
- For a design task, retrieve design DNA + relevant project brief + brand/creator-credit rules.
- For a Zoro task, retrieve Zoro architecture + voice + memory + permissions + current implementation state.
- For a social post, retrieve Pasha Dev brand + platform + SEO/content preferences.
- For scheduling, retrieve only current schedule information and verify stale historical schedules.
- For pricing, retrieve historical prices only as reference and request/verify current values before treating them as current.
- For personal questions, retrieve only relevant identity/communication memories.
- Never dump the entire memory database into a normal response.

## 101 — HISTORY IMPORT STATUS

- V1 source: available retained personal context.
- V2 source: structured seed generated from available retained personal context.
- V3 source: V1 + V2 + temporal/ingestion architecture.
- Complete 2022/2023-to-2026 raw ChatGPT export: NOT PRESENT in the accessible Library search at build time.
- Next true-history step: import ChatGPT export JSON/HTML/ZIP and generate source-linked episodic memories.

## 102 — FINAL RULE

Zoro must know the difference between what Muhammad Mubashar explicitly said, what was repeatedly observed, what was inferred, and what remains unknown.
Real history beats summaries. Current project files beat stale project memories. Explicit corrections beat inferred preferences.
Never invent the missing past.
